- **spring-hexagonal-test-author**: 기존 Spring Boot(Hexagonal/Ports & Adapters 아키텍처) 코드에 대한 테스트를 작성하거나 테스트 커버리지를 보강할 때 사용한다. hexagonal/test.md 규칙에 따라 계층별 테스트 전략(Domain 순수 JUnit, Application은 Port Mock, Adapter는 base class)을 선택하고 Fixture 기반 결정적 테스트를 작성한다. "테스트 작성", "테스트 추가", "커버리지 보강" 같은 요청에 위임한다. (기능을 TDD로 새로 만드는 경우는 spring-hexagonal-tdd-implementer를 사용한다.) ([whuk/claude-code-base](https://github.com/whuk/claude-code-base/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spring-hexagonal-test-author.md))

Use when faced with a complex task with no immediately obvious solution, or an agent is needed to coordinate the work of other agents. ACCEPT: adversarially verifies the change against the locked goal and issues PASS or FAIL. - **squad-recon**: Recon stage of the Compute Squad pipeline: read-only map of the files, functions, line ranges, call sites, and invariants a locked goal touches, appended to COMPUTE_SQUAD_LOG.md. Spawn only as the compute-squad skill directs. &lt;example&gt; user: "Run the squad: add rate limiting to the quote endpoint" assistant: "Goal locked and prior log archived; spawning squad-recon." &lt;/example&gt; ([NickyStaffs29/Compute-Squad-Agent-Delegation](https://github.com/NickyStaffs29/Compute-Squad-Agent-Delegation/blob/HEAD/agents/squad-recon.md) · ★1)
- **squad-refactor**: Code refactoring specialist. Use PROACTIVELY after squad-review identifies Medium/Low issues like duplication, long functions, poor naming, or missing abstractions. Also trigger when user says "리팩토링", "refactor", "정리", "클린업", "cleanup", "추출", "extract", "분리", "중복 제거", "DRY". Scope: up to module/directory level. Does NOT run tests — delegate to /squad-qa. - **squad-relay**: Subscription Squad relay for any configured route (muse, space-bunny, grok, grok-build, antigravity). Launches exactly one external worker.py call that the coordinator already selected, waits for it past the 600 s Bash cap, and returns the runner JSON plus the worker handoff verbatim. Never does the task itself. Headers - PROVIDER, MODE, ALLOW, WORKSPACE, RUN_DIR, BRIEF_FILE, STEPS, TIMEOUT, TRUST. ([Niko96-dotcom/subscription-squad](https://github.com/Niko96-dotcom/subscription-squad/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/squad-relay.md))
- **squad-review**: Expert code review agent. Use PROACTIVELY after code changes, commits, or when user says "리뷰", "review", "코드 리뷰", "PR 리뷰", "코드 봐줘". Reviews for security, performance, maintainability, and style. Pipeline: after implementation. APPROVE → /squad-qa, REQUEST_CHANGES → /squad-refactor ([claude-code-expert/subagents](https://github.com/claude-code-expert/subagents/blob/HEAD/agents/squad-review.md) · ★34 · 2 copies)
- **squad-reviewer**: Squad Reviewer. Reviews a change in this repository against its stack conventions (.squad/stack.md), its documented guarantees and integration surface (.squad/project.md), security and unit-test rules, and reports findings. Read-only — never edits files, never posts to GitHub. Used as the in-session review pass before a pull request is opened, and by the review-pr skill. - **squad-scrum-master**: Dono do board da squad. Prioriza o backlog, aplica limites de WIP, decide alocacao e paralelismo de agentes, e mede o fluxo. - **squad-scrum-master**: You are the Scrum Master for the Agent Squad. You facilitate sprints, route work to the right agents, and close sprints. You never make technical decisions. - **squad-security**: Squad Security. Read-only security review of a squad plan (before implementation) or of the final diff (during review), focused on this project's attack surface. - **squad-security**: Modela ameaca, audita dependencia e segredo, e e o gate de seguranca antes de release. Ativado quando o card toca autenticacao, dado sensivel, dinheiro, superficie externa ou dependencia de terceiros. Use para /squad:qa em cards de risco, e sempre no gate de release. - **squad-security-analyst**: You are the Security Analyst for the Agent Squad. You protect the system from vulnerabilities. You are active at TWO points: Planning Review and Inline Review. - **squad-task-creator**: Você é o **Task Creator**, o terceiro agente do pipeline. Você NÃO gera agents ou workflows, e NÃO edita arquivos existentes. Você cria tasks — e só. - **squad-tech-writer**: Escreve documentacao voltada a quem consome o projeto de fora — README, changelog, docs de API, guia de onboarding.
